Now the deobfuscation logic in util.py:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01VM2ppXU5PMb6hP1Nr4FqwU
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/raman/projects/lock_code_manager/custom_components/lock_code_manager/domain/util.py",
  "old_string": "def mask_pin(\n    pin: str | None,\n    slot_num: int | str,\n    instance_id: str,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a deterministic masked representation of a PIN for logging.\n\n    Uses CRC32 salted with the HA instance ID and slot number so the same\n    PIN on the same slot always produces the same 8-char hex token regardless\n    of which lock or config entry it belongs to.  Different slots or HA\n    instances produce different tokens.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not pin:\n        return \"<empty>\"\n    salt = f\"{instance_id}:{slot_num}:{pin}\"\n    return f\"pin#{zlib.crc32(salt.encode()) & 0xFFFFFFFF:08x}\"",
  "new_string": "def mask_pin(\n    pin: str | None,\n    slot_num: int | str,\n    instance_id: str,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a deterministic masked representation of a PIN for logging.\n\n    Uses CRC32 salted with the HA instance ID and slot number so the same\n    PIN on the same slot always produces the same 8-char hex token regardless\n    of which lock or config entry it belongs to.  Different slots or HA\n    instances produce different tokens.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not pin:\n        return \"<empty>\"\n    salt = f\"{instance_id}:{slot_num}:{pin}\"\n    return f\"pin#{zlib.crc32(salt.encode()) & 0xFFFFFFFF:08x}\"\n\n\n# Token format emitted by ``mask_pin``: literal \"pin#\" followed by 8 lowercase\n# hex chars. Anchored by word boundary on the right so a longer hex string\n# isn't truncated and reported as a match.\n_PIN_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r\"pin#[0-9a-f]{8}\\b\")\n\n\ndef build_pin_deobfuscation_map(\n    entries: Iterable[ConfigEntry], instance_id: str\n) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build a ``{masked_token: plaintext_pin}`` lookup for every configured PIN.\n\n    Reads each entry's options-or-data view via ``EntryConfig`` so it works\n    whether or not the entry has been migrated to options yet. Slots whose\n    PIN is empty are skipped because ``mask_pin`` returns ``<empty>`` for\n    them and that's not a token we need to reverse.\n\n    Different ``(slot, pin)`` pairs always produce different tokens (slot\n    is part of the salt), so the resulting map has one entry per\n    configured slot with a PIN.\n    \"\"\"\n    table: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for entry in entries:\n        config = EntryConfig.from_entry(entry)\n        for slot_num, slot_config in config.slots.items():\n            pin = slot_config.get(CONF_PIN)\n            if not pin:\n                continue\n            table[mask_pin(pin, slot_num, instance_id)] = pin\n    return table\n\n\ndef deobfuscate_pins(\n    text: str, table: dict[str, str]\n) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Replace ``pin#xxxxxxxx`` tokens in ``text`` using ``table``.\n\n    Tokens not in the table are left verbatim so the output stays\n    paste-compatible with the original log (useful when only some PINs\n    have been rotated since the log was written). The summary lists the\n    distinct unmatched tokens so the caller can see what didn't resolve\n    without scanning the text.\n    \"\"\"\n    counts = {\"total\": 0, \"matched\": 0}\n    unmatched: set[str] = set()\n\n    def _replace(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        token = match.group(0)\n        counts[\"total\"] += 1\n        if token in table:\n            counts[\"matched\"] += 1\n            return table[token]\n        unmatched.add(token)\n        return token\n\n    deobfuscated = _PIN_TOKEN_RE.sub(_replace, text)\n    summary: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"total\": counts[\"total\"],\n        \"matched\": counts[\"matched\"],\n        \"unmatched_tokens\": sorted(unmatched),\n    }\n    return deobfuscated, summary"
}

  "new_string": "    async def _generate_pin(call: ServiceCall) -> ServiceResponse:\n        \"\"\"Generate a random PIN that avoids known unsafe patterns.\"\"\"\n        return {\"pin\": generate_pin(call.data[ATTR_LENGTH])}\n\n    hass.services.async_register(\n        DOMAIN,\n        SERVICE_GENERATE_PIN,\n        _generate_pin,\n        schema=vol.Schema(\n            {\n                vol.Optional(ATTR_LENGTH, default=DEFAULT_PIN_LENGTH): vol.All(\n                    vol.Coerce(int), vol.Range(min=MIN_PIN_LENGTH, max=MAX_PIN_LENGTH)\n                ),\n            }\n        ),\n        supports_response=SupportsResponse.ONLY,\n    )\n\n    async def _deobfuscate_log(call: ServiceCall) -> ServiceResponse:\n        \"\"\"Reverse mask_pin() tokens in pasted log text against the current config.\"\"\"\n        instance_id = hass.data.get(DOMAIN, {}).get(\"instance_id\", \"\")\n        if not instance_id:\n            raise HomeAssistantError(\n                \"Lock Code Manager is not fully set up yet; try again in a moment\"\n            )\n        entries = hass.config_entries.async_loaded_entries(DOMAIN)\n        table = build_pin_deobfuscation_map(entries, instance_id)\n        deobfuscated, summary = deobfuscate_pins(call.data[\"log_text\"], table)\n        # Sentinel banner so users see at a glance that the response contains\n        # plaintext PINs and must not be pasted into a public issue.\n        wrapped = (\n            \"=== BEGIN DEOBFUSCATED — DO NOT SHARE ===\\n\"\n            f\"{deobfuscated}\\n\"\n            \"=== END DEOBFUSCATED ===\"\n        )\n        return {\"deobfuscated_text\": wrapped, \"summary\": summary}\n\n    hass.services.async_register(\n        DOMAIN,\n        SERVICE_DEOBFUSCATE_LOG,\n        _deobfuscate_log,\n        schema=vol.Schema({vol.Required(\"log_text\"): cv.string}),\n        supports_response=SupportsResponse.ONLY,\n    )\n\n    return True"
}

23	    """Test mask_pin returns a pin# prefixed 8-char lowercase hex hash."""

25	    assert re.fullmatch(r"pin#[0-9a-f]{8}", result), f"Unexpected format: {result}"

BASE_CONFIG has slot 1 with PIN \"1234\" and slot 2 with PIN \"5678\".\n    token_slot_1 = mask_pin(\"1234\", 1, instance_id)\n    token_slot_2 = mask_pin(\"5678\", 2, instance_id)\n    log_excerpt = (\n        f\"Setting usercode on lock.test_1 slot 1 (pin={token_slot_1})\\n\"\n        f\"Setting usercode on lock.test_2 slot 2 (pin={token_slot_2})\\n\"\n        \"Unrelated line with no token\\n\"\n        \"Stale token from a rotated PIN: pin#deadbeef\"\n    )\n\n    response = await hass.services.async_call(\n        DOMAIN,\n        SERVICE_DEOBFUSCATE_LOG,\n        {ATTR_TEXT: log_excerpt},\n        blocking=True,\n        return_response=True,\n    )\n\n    assert response is not None\n    text = response[\"deobfuscated_text\"]\n    assert \"1234\" in text\n    assert \"5678\" in text\n    assert token_slot_1 not in text\n    assert token_slot_2 not in text\n    # Stale token is preserved verbatim so the output remains comparable to the input.\n    assert \"pin#deadbeef\" in text\n    assert \"BEGIN DEOBFUSCATED\" in text and \"END DEOBFUSCATED\" in text\n\n    summary = response[\"summary\"]\n    assert summary == {\n        \"total\": 3,\n        \"matched\": 2,\n        \"unmatched_tokens\": [\"pin#deadbeef\"],\n    }"

{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat: add deobfuscate_log service to reverse masked PIN tokens in logs\n\nmask_pin() emits \"pin#xxxxxxxx\" tokens in log lines so users can share\nlogs without leaking PINs. The reverse direction is also useful for\nlocal debugging — a user pasting their own logs to see which slot a\nwarning refers to. Build the inverse: enumerate every (slot, pin) pair\nacross all loaded LCM entries, precompute mask_pin for each, and\nsubstitute matches in pasted text.\n\nNew service ``lock_code_manager.deobfuscate_log`` (response-only):\n- Input: ``text`` — pasted log content\n- Output:\n  - ``deobfuscated_text``: input with tokens replaced, wrapped in\n    \"=== BEGIN DEOBFUSCATED — DO NOT SHARE ===\" sentinel so users\n    notice the response contains plaintext PINs before pasting it\n    anywhere shared\n  - ``summary``: ``{total, matched, unmatched_tokens}``\n\nUnmatched tokens (PINs rotated since the log was written, or from\nother instances) are preserved verbatim so the output stays\npaste-compatible with the original log. They are listed in the\nsummary so the caller can see what didn't resolve.\n\nThe masking uses CRC32 (not cryptographic), so this service does not\nchange the threat model — a motivated attacker who knows the salt\nformat can already brute-force 4-12 digit PINs against the tokens in\nmilliseconds. The service is for the legitimate user's own debugging.\n\nTests cover the lookup builder, token substitution (matched/unmatched/\nmixed/repeated/partial tokens), summary correctness, and the\nend-to-end service round trip.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit"
}

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"feat: add deobfuscate_log service to reverse masked PIN tokens\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Proposed change\n\n`mask_pin()` emits `pin#xxxxxxxx` tokens in log lines so users can share logs without leaking PINs. The reverse direction is also useful for **local** debugging — a user pasting their own logs to see which slot a warning actually refers to. This PR adds a service that does exactly that.\n\n### Service: `lock_code_manager.deobfuscate_log`\n\n**Input**:\n- `text` (required, multiline string) — pasted log content\n\n**Output (`SupportsResponse.ONLY`)**:\n- `deobfuscated_text` — input with every `pin#xxxxxxxx` token whose plaintext is in the current configuration replaced by the real PIN, wrapped in `=== BEGIN DEOBFUSCATED — DO NOT SHARE ===` / `=== END DEOBFUSCATED ===` sentinels so users notice the response contains plaintext PINs before pasting it anywhere shared\n- `summary` — `{total, matched, unmatched_tokens}`\n\n### How it works\n\n`build_pin_deobfuscation_map` iterates every loaded LCM config entry, reads its slot config via the existing `EntryConfig` view, and computes `mask_pin(pin, slot_num, instance_id)` for every slot with a non-empty PIN. The result is a `{token: pin}` dict the service uses for substitution. CRC32 is fast — building the map for hundreds of slots takes microseconds.\n\n`deobfuscate_pins` scans for `pin#[0-9a-f]{8}\\b` (word-boundary anchored so a longer hex string isn't truncated and falsely matched) and substitutes. **Unmatched tokens are left verbatim** so the output stays paste-compatible with the original log; the summary lists them.\n\n### Security note\n\nThe masking uses **CRC32**, not a cryptographic hash. This service doesn't change the threat model — an attacker who knows the salt format can already brute-force 4-12 digit PINs against the tokens in milliseconds. The service is for the legitimate user's own debugging, and the sentinel banner + service description make clear the response must not be pasted into public issues.\n\n### Tests\n\n- `test_deobfuscate_pins_replaces_known_tokens` — known matched, unknown preserved, summary correct\n- `test_deobfuscate_pins_no_tokens_in_text` — empty summary when no tokens\n- `test_deobfuscate_pins_counts_each_occurrence` — same token replaced multiple times, counted\n- `test_deobfuscate_pins_unmatched_tokens_deduplicated` — distinct unmatched in summary\n- `test_deobfuscate_pins_does_not_match_partial_tokens` — wrong-length and non-hex rejected\n- `test_build_pin_deobfuscation_map_uses_configured_pins` — map matches mask_pin output for `BASE_CONFIG`\n- `test_deobfuscate_log_service_round_trips_configured_pins` — end-to-end through the service registry\n\nAll 963 tests pass.\n\n## Type of change\n\n- [ ] Dependency upgrade\n- [ ] Bugfix (non-breaking change which fixes an issue)\n- [x] New feature (which adds functionality)\n- [ ] Breaking change (fix/feature causing existing functionality to break)\n- [ ] Code quality improvements to existing code or addition of tests\n\n## Additional information\n\n- This PR fixes or closes issue:\n- This PR is related to issue:\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -3",
  "description": "Open PR"
}

PR #1217 opened: https://github.com/raman325/lock_code_manager/pull/1217

## Summary

Two PRs out:
- **#1216** — chore: fill in missing entity and service icons
- **#1217** — feat: add `deobfuscate_log` service to reverse masked PIN tokens

The deobfuscation flow lands as: paste a log into Dev Tools → Services → `lock_code_manager.deobfuscate_log` → response shows tokens substituted with the configured-PIN plaintext, wrapped in `=== BEGIN DEOBFUSCATED — DO NOT SHARE ===` sentinels so it's hard to accidentally leak. Unmatched tokens (rotated PINs, other instances) are preserved verbatim so the output stays paste-compatible. 963 tests pass.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The word boundary in the regex (`pin#[0-9a-f]{8}\b`) is doing real work — without it, a token like `pin#abcdef0123456789` (16 hex chars) would falsely match its first 8 chars and the response would smuggle in stray output. Small detail; verified via the `test_deobfuscate_pins_does_not_match_partial_tokens` test.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

Resolved. Conflict was the simple union of two `"services"` blocks — kept all 7 entries alphabetical, no logic touched. `--force-with-lease` (not `--force`) so the push would have refused if someone had pushed to the branch between my fetch and my push.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`--force-with-lease` is the safer cousin of `--force`: it sends along the SHA you *expect* to overwrite, and if the remote has moved past that, the push fails instead of clobbering someone else's commits. Always prefer it over `--force` for branches even you alone work on — the cost is zero, the saved incident is potentially large.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

PR #1217 should now re-run CI on the rebased branch.

## Pull request overview

Adds a Home Assistant service (`lock_code_manager.deobfuscate_log`) that reverses `mask_pin()` log tokens back to plaintext PINs for local debugging, using the currently loaded Lock Code Manager configuration.

**Changes:**
- Implemented `build_pin_deobfuscation_map()` and `deobfuscate_pins()` utilities and registered the `deobfuscate_log` service (with sentinel wrappers and a summary response).
- Added service UI/metadata (strings/translations, service schema YAML, icon).
- Added unit + service-level tests covering token matching/replacement behavior and end-to-end service response.

### Reviewed changes

Copilot reviewed 9 out of 9 changed files in this pull request and generated 1 comment.

| `custom_components/lock_code_manager/translations/en.json` | Adds English translations for the new service strings. |
| `custom_components/lock_code_manager/icons.json` | Assigns an icon to the new service. |
| `tests/test_util.py` | Adds unit tests for deobfuscation logic + map building. |
| `tests/test_services.py` | Adds end-to-end test for the service response and sentinel wrapping. |

Copilot's review left one inline comment on `util.py:60-62`:

> The docstring claims different (slot, pin) pairs "always" produce different tokens, but mask_pin uses CRC32 so collisions are theoretically possible. The docs should avoid absolute guarantees to prevent misleading future maintainers/users.

Fair and technically correct — CRC32's 32-bit output means collisions are mathematically possible (probability is ~10⁻⁷ for 100 pairs by birthday math, but not zero). Easy fix to the docstring:

> AGENT

Done. One Copilot inline comment — accurate point about overstated certainty. Pushed `0728f335` rewording the docstring from "always produce different tokens" to "produce different tokens with overwhelming probability" with the CRC32 caveat. Replied to the comment thread on the PR.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
Worth taking even when it's a bot: docstring "always" / "never" claims are load-bearing for future maintainers debugging a weird incident. If someone *did* see two slots resolve to the same plaintext PIN years from now, the docstring saying "always different" would send them down a wrong-cause investigation. Hedging is essentially free.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`
